This paper presents lessons learned from evaluating six large language models for AI-driven workflow generation in a production enterprise platform, benchmarked across 29 real-world IT automation scenarios, two generation pipeline architectures, and eight independent runs per prompt-model-pipeline configuration (2,784 runs total).
  Our initial pipeline used monolithic workflow generation, achieving 31.5-82.8% structural success rates (JSON schema validity and correct UI rendering), with most models struggling on complex JSON generation. We developed a redesigned piecewise pipeline that decomposes workflow construction into variable scaffolding, base block assembly, and nested block generation, raising structural success to 74.1-97.8% across all models.
  We analyze production tradeoffs including cost (USD 0.008-0.20 per workflow), latency (under 50s for interactive use), and model selection. Piecewise decomposition enables smaller models (e.g., mistral-small at 95.7% structural success and USD 0.01 per workflow) to reach production viability, removing dependency on expensive frontier models. While mistral-medium-2505 and gpt-oss-120b achieved the highest structural success (96.1% and 97.8%), mistral-medium-2505 carries a 19x cost premium versus mistral-small. Our deployment lessons highlight the need to separate structural validity from semantic correctness (logical fulfillment of user intent) and provide a solution for model-agnostic, scalable automation in cloud engineering.

</details>


### [243] [Making AI Visible, Not Vanished: How AI Policies Reshape Developer Experience on GitHub](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03329)
*Yunqi Chen,Thomas Zimmermann,Bianca Trinkenreich*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文首次大规模实证研究开源软件（OSS）社区中的AI治理政策。通过分析29,624个GitHub仓库，识别出385个采用AI政策的项目，提出TRACE框架（涵盖透明度、责任、归属、约束和执行五个治理维度），并利用倾向评分匹配和纵向双重差分法评估政策影响。研究发现AI治理主要规范而非禁止AI辅助开发，且强调透明度与责任的政策比单纯限制性政策带来更好的社区和质量成果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI正在快速改变OSS开发模式，许多项目开始引入AI贡献治理政策，但我们对这些政策之间的差异及其对开发者体验的影响知之甚少。缺乏系统性的政策分类框架和实证依据，难以指导OSS社区制定有效的AI治理策略。

Method: 研究者收集并分析了29,624个GitHub仓库，从中识别出385个采用AI政策的项目。提出TRACE框架用于描述政策在透明度、责任、归属、约束和执行五个维度上的特征，将政策分类为五种治理家族。采用倾向评分匹配控制选择偏差，并结合纵向双重差分法估计政策采纳的因果效应。

Result: 研究发现AI治理政策主要起到规范而非禁止AI辅助开发的作用。政策采纳带来了维护者参与度提升、AI披露增加、更丰富的审查互动以及代码质量改善，同时AI辅助贡献持续增长。强调透明度和责任维度的政策在社区活跃度和质量成果上优于单纯限制性的政策。

Conclusion: 不同AI治理策略对开发者体验产生显著差异化的影响，透明度和责任导向的政策设计比限制性政策更有效。研究为OSS社区设计有效的AI治理政策提供了实证依据和分类框架。

Abstract: Generative AI is rapidly reshaping Open Source Software (OSS) software development,prompting projects to introduce policies governing AI-assisted contributions. However, little is known about how these policies differ or whether they influence developer experience. We present the first large-scale empirical study of AI governance policies in OSS. Analyzing 29,624 GitHub repositories, we identify 385 projects that adopted AI policies and derive TRACE, a framework capturing five governance dimensions: Transparency, Responsibility, Attribution, Constraints, and Enforcement. We further classify policies into five governance families and estimate their effects using propensity-score matching and longitudinal difference-in-differences analysis. Our results show that AI governance primarily regulates rather than prohibits AI-assisted development. Policy adoption brings maintainer engagement, increased AI disclosure, richer review interactions, and improved code quality while AI-assisted contributions continue to grow. Governance design matters: policies emphasizing transparency and responsibility produced stronger community and quality outcomes than restrictive approaches alone. Our findings show how different AI governance strategies shape developer experience and provide evidence to help OSS communities design effective AI policies.

</details>


### [244] [Assessing Behavioral Validation in UI Component Test Suites Using Inferred Metamorphic Relations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03337)
*Yu Pei,Cunming Zhang,Jeongju Sohn,Mike Papadakis*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于变质关系（MR）的框架，用于评估UI组件测试套件的行为验证质量，弥补传统执行覆盖率指标的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于语句和分支覆盖率等执行指标无法充分反映测试是否真正验证了组件API和文档所隐含的行为关系，缺乏对测试行为验证质量的深入洞见。

Method: 该框架通过UI特定分类法推断组件特定的变质关系，结合确定性分析与语义分析将测试与推断关系对齐，并计算关系级别的MR覆盖率指标，同时通过手动验证确保推断和对齐的可靠性。

Result: 评估显示现有测试套件执行的行为关系远多于其显式验证的：MR Cover维持在42.5%-47.6%之间且始终低于MR Touch；大多数未覆盖关系属于弱oracle情况；MR覆盖率能揭示语句/分支覆盖率无法反映的行为缺口；大多数问题描述可映射到推断的MR关系类型。

Conclusion: MR覆盖率提供了一种补充性的关系级视角，用于评估现代UI组件测试中的行为验证质量。

Abstract: UI component libraries are commonly assessed using execution-based metrics such as statement and branch coverage, yet these metrics provide limited insight into whether tests verify the behavioral relations implied by component APIs and documentation. This paper presents an MR-based framework that uses inferred metamorphic relations (MRs) as an empirical behavioral reference, rather than a complete specification, for assessing UI component test suites. Given a component's source, documentation, and tests, the framework infers component-specific MRs using a UI-specific taxonomy, aligns tests with the inferred relations through hybrid deterministic and semantic analysis, and computes relation-level MR coverage metrics. We manually validate both the inferred MR space and the test--MR alignment. Our evaluation shows that existing test suites exercise substantially more behavioral relations than they explicitly validate: MR Cover remains between 42.5% and 47.6% across three LLM configurations and consistently below MR Touch. Most uncovered relations are weak-oracle cases, where behaviors are exercised but lack explicit behavioral validation. MR coverage also complements execution-based coverage by revealing behavioral gaps not reflected by statement or branch coverage alone. We further assess practical relevance through issue-description mapping, oracle strengthening, and MR-relevant injected faults. Most reported issue descriptions can be mapped to inferred MR relation types; weak-oracle relations often expose missing validation evidence; and MR labels show a trend in MR-relevant fault detection. Overall, MR coverage provides a complementary relation-level perspective for assessing behavioral validation in modern UI component testing.

</details>


### [245] [Route-Align-Verify for Functional Correctness in Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03341)
*Erxue Zhou,Jingxiang Meng,Aofan Liu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出RAV框架，通过三个协调阶段（Route路由、Align对齐、Verify验证）在固定骨干模型上提升代码生成的功能正确性，在MBPP基准上取得显著改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型在代码生成上虽有进步，但面对异构编程任务时，单一提示策略和单一直接生成输出往往不足以保证功能正确性，需要更系统的框架来提升性能。

Method: RAV框架包含三个阶段：(1) Route——在生成前进行任务感知的提示路由；(2) Align——通过对齐的LoRA适配减少微调提示与推理时提示之间的不匹配；(3) Verify——通过执行多个候选输出并对照可见公共测试来选择最终结果。

Result: 在MBPP基准的sanitized和full设置下，完整RAV管道分别达到0.8911和0.8520，相比基础模型分别提升6.35和9.92个百分点。消融实验表明任务感知路由和对齐适配在与基于执行的验证结合时效果显著增强。

Conclusion: 通过联合优化提示方式、模型适配和输出选择，可以在不修改骨干架构的情况下有效提升代码生成的功能正确性，验证了RAV框架的可靠性和有效性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have substantially improved code generation, yet achieving strong functional correctness remains difficult, especially for heterogeneous programming tasks where a single prompting strategy and a single directly generated output are often insufficient. In this paper, we present RAV, a lightweight and modular framework that improves code generation with a fixed backbone model through three coordinated stages: Route, which applies task-aware prompt routing before generation; Align, which reduces the mismatch between fine-tuning prompts and inference-time prompts through aligned LoRA adaptation; and Verify, which selects the final output by executing multiple candidates against visible public tests.
  We evaluate RAV on the MBPP benchmark under both the sanitized and full settings. The complete RAV pipeline achieves the best performance among all evaluated configurations, reaching 0.8911 on MBPP Sanitized and 0.8520 on MBPP Full. Compared with the base model, these results represent improvements of 6.35 and 9.92 percentage points, respectively. Component-wise ablation experiments further show that task-aware routing and aligned adaptation become substantially more effective when combined with execution-based verification. Additional robustness and contamination analyses support the reliability of the observed improvements. Overall, the results indicate that functional correctness in code generation can be meaningfully improved without modifying the backbone architecture, by jointly optimizing how tasks are prompted, how the model is adapted, and how final outputs are selected.

</details>


### [246] [Self-Evolving Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03392)
*Hao Zhou,Haichuan Hu,Ye Shang,Quanjun Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文是对自我进化编码代理（self-evolving coding agents）这一新兴领域的系统性综述，定义了该概念，提出了以"对象为中心"的分类体系，并从时间和证据两个正交视角进行补充分析，总结了软件工程领域作为代理自我进化自然场景的优势与挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型已被广泛用作编码代理，但大多数现有代理在部署后保持静态，无法适应软件开发的动态和反馈丰富过程（如仓库演化、依赖变化、测试失败等）。作者希望系统化梳理这个快速发展但缺乏统一框架的新兴研究领域。

Method: 首先明确定义自我进化编码代理，并将其与传统编码代理及通用自我进化代理区分开来；然后提出一个以对象为中心的分类体系（characterizes what evolves），并用两个正交视角补充：何时发生进化（when）以及什么软件特定证据驱动进化（what software-specific evidence drives it）；最后对收集的文献进行系统性整理与组织。

Result: 研究发现可执行反馈、仓库级上下文和编码轨迹使软件工程成为代理自我进化的天然领域，但同时也引入了反馈可靠性、基准过拟合、安全性、可维护性、成本和泛化等新挑战。论文提供了一个公开的论文集合仓库。

Conclusion: 该综述旨在澄清自我进化编码代理的概念边界，并为设计更自适应、可靠且具软件意识的代理系统奠定基础。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly embedded in software engineering workflows as coding agents that can inspect repositories, invoke tools, execute tests, debug failures, and generate patches. Yet most existing agents remain largely static after deployment, even though software development is a dynamic, feedback-rich process in which repositories evolve, dependencies change, tests fail, and repair attempts leave reusable experience. This tension has motivated a growing body of work on self-evolving coding agents, where the agent improves its future behavior by updating its framework, memory, skills, tools, models, or collaboration structures from prior coding interactions. In this survey, we provide a systematic synthesis of this emerging area. We first define self-evolving coding agents and distinguish them from conventional coding agents and general self-evolving agents. We then develop an object-centered taxonomy that characterizes what evolves in these systems, and complement it with two orthogonal perspectives: when evolution occurs and what software-specific evidence drives it. Across the literature, we find that executable feedback, repository-level context, and coding trajectories give software engineering a distinctive role as a natural domain for agent self-evolution, but also introduce new challenges in feedback reliability, benchmark overfitting, safety, maintainability, cost, and generalization. By organizing existing work around these dimensions, this survey aims to clarify the conceptual boundaries of self-evolving coding agents and provide a foundation for designing more adaptive, reliable, and software-aware agentic systems. The papers we collect can be found at https://github.com/zhouhao1024/Awesome-Self-Evolving-Coding-Agents.

</details>


### [247] [When AI Joins the Team! A Model of How AI Adoption Relates To Social Patterns in Software Engineering Teams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03462)
*Giusy Annunziata,Rudrajit Choudhuri,Anita Sarma,Gemma Catolino,Filomena Ferrucci*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究基于Transactive Memory Systems (TMS)理论，通过对152名使用AI工具的软件专业人员调研，发现AI采用与社区坏味道（community smells）的关系并非单一，而是取决于工作类型：在专业化工作中，AI通过提升同伴知识共享互动间接减少坏味道；在协调工作中，AI直接提升沟通质量，补充而非替代人际互动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI辅助开发工具的普及正在改变软件团队的协作、知识共享与协调方式，但其对团队社会动态（social dynamics）的影响尚不清楚。现有研究缺乏对AI采用与社区坏味道（反映协调与沟通断裂的社会技术反模式）之间关系的实证考察，也缺乏对其作用机制的深入理解。

Method: 以Transactive Memory Systems（TMS）理论为基础，构建并验证了沿TMS两个维度（专业化Specialization与协调Coordination）的Human-AI和Human-Human互动测量工具；社区坏味道构念从文献中提取并通过专家调查和因子分析验证；基于152名使用AI工具的软件专业人员调查数据，检验了五个PLS-SEM结构方程模型。

Result: AI采用与社区坏味道之间并非单一对应关系，而是通过取决于工作类型的机制产生作用。在专业化工作中，AI与更高的知识共享同伴互动相关，进而与更少的坏味道相关；在协调工作中，AI直接与更高的沟通质量相关，起到补充而非替代人类互动的作用。

Conclusion: 研究提供了一个经验验证的、基于TMS理论的模型，表明AI与社区坏味道的关系取决于协作类型。同时提供了可复用的测量工具以及面向研究与实践的循证启示，填补了AI采用对团队社会动态影响这一领域的研究空白。

Abstract: Context: The growing adoption of AI-assisted development tools is changing how software teams collaborate, share knowledge, and coordinate, yet its consequences for team social dynamics remain largely unexplored. Gap: It is unclear whether AI adoption is associated with an increase or reduction in community smells,socio-technical anti-patterns reflecting coordination and communication breakdowns,and through which mechanisms. Method: Grounded in Transactive Memory Systems (TMS) theory, we validate instruments for HumanAI and HumanHuman interaction along two TMS dimensions, Specialization and Coordination, and test five PLS-SEM models on survey data from 152 software professionals using AI tools. Community smell constructs were derived from the literature and validated through expert surveys and factor analysis. Results: AI adoption relates to community smells not in a single way, but through mechanisms depending on the work. In specialization work, AI is associated with higher knowledge-sharing peer interaction, which is in turn associated with fewer smells. In coordination work, AI is directly associated with higher communication quality, complementing rather than replacing human interaction. Contributions: We provide an empirically validated, TMS-grounded model showing that the AIcommunity-smell relationship is contingent on the type of collaboration, with a reusable instrument and evidence-based implications for research and practice.

</details>


### [248] [Leveraging System-Level Observations to Inform Bayesian Learning of Model Parameters for Quantitative Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03489)
*Simos Gerasimou,Xingyu Zhao*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: EPIK方法通过结合贝叶斯学习和定量验证，利用系统级可观测属性来提取和嵌入先验知识（PK），通过双重优化问题推导未知转移参数的分布，从而提升量化验证的准确性和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贝叶斯学习结合定量验证是分析软件系统可靠性、响应时间等关键量化属性的强大工具，但验证结果的准确性和鲁棒性高度依赖于贝叶斯推断中的先验知识（PK）。不准确或无信息的PK会导致错误的验证结果。现有方法需要关于形式模型转移参数的PK，这在实际中难以获取且常依赖领域专家经验。

Method: EPIK方法从系统级可观测属性中提取PK（这些属性与真实世界语义直接相关），然后将其嵌入到配备贝叶斯估计器的定量验证中。具体地，EPIK构建一个双重优化问题来推导未知转移参数的分布，再将这些分布嵌入以验证新的或难以测量的（ elusive）属性。

Result: 通过在多个真实世界案例研究的多种变体和多样的EPIK实例化上的详细实验评估，证明了该方法在有效性、灵活性和通用性方面的优势。

Conclusion: EPIK提供了一种新颖且实用的方式来处理定量验证中的先验知识提取难题，相比现有依赖形式模型转移参数PK的方法，它从可观察的系统级属性出发，更具现实可行性和广泛适用性。

Abstract: Combining Bayesian learning and quantitative verification is a powerful toolset for analysing key quantitative properties of software systems, like reliability and response time. However, the accuracy and robustness of verification results strongly depend on the prior knowledge (PK) underlying Bayesian inference. This knowledge reflects original beliefs about the probability of events and typically depends on domain expertise. Using inaccurate or uninformative PK can negatively affect quantitative analysis, yielding incorrect verification results. Our EPIK approach tackles this important challenge by eliciting and embedding PK in quantitative verification equipped with Bayesian estimators. Unlike existing approaches that require PK on formal model transition parameters, EPIK leverages system-level properties that are directly observable and are linked to real-world semantics. EPIK formulates a twofold optimisation problem to derive the distributions of unknown transition parameters and then embeds these distributions to verify new or difficult-to-measure (elusive) properties. The detailed experimental evaluation using multiple variants of real-world case studies and diverse EPIK instantiations shows its effectiveness, flexibility and generality.

</details>


### [249] [CodeAssay: A Multi-Metric Benchmark with Audited Ground Truth for LLM Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03535)
*Shahbaz Siddeeq,Muhammad Waseem,Umar Subhan Malhi,Pekka Abrahamsson*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出CodeAssay，一个基于分类法的Python代码生成基准测试，包含185个任务、10个软件工程类别，结合审计过的ground truth、公开/隐藏测试、变异测试验证和代码属性度量。研究发现：审计后修正了9.0%的正确性标签，将模型间差距从11.9个百分点扩大到23.7个百分点；安全提示未显著改变正确性，反而增加了程序复杂度和长度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM代码生成评估高度依赖基于测试的基准，但此类评估的有效性取决于参考答案和测试的可靠性，且测试只能捕获生成代码的可观察属性的部分。研究者希望构建一个更可靠、更全面、可复现的评估框架。

Method: 构建包含185个Python任务、覆盖十个软件工程类别的分类法优先基准；采用审计过的ground truth、公开测试（生成与修复）、隐藏测试（评分）、基于变异的测试套件验证，以及多种代码属性度量。对7个专有LLM和14个模型-提示配置进行系统评估。

Result: 审计修正了1890个正确性标签中的170个（9.0%），将模型间最优与最差差距从11.9扩大到23.7个百分点；完整和隐藏测试套件的变异分数分别为82.6%和74.8%；标准提示下正确率范围为77.3%-98.9%，21对模型中有12对存在显著差异；安全提示未显著改变正确性，但所有模型都增加了程序长度和圈复杂度。

Conclusion: 可靠的LLM代码生成评估需要经过验证的ground truth、受保护的测试以及多重明确解释的度量标准。CodeAssay为AI辅助软件开发中的循证模型评估提供了可复现的基础。

Abstract: Large Language Models are increasingly evaluated for code generation using test-based benchmarks. The validity of such evaluations depends on the reliability of their references and tests, while test-based correctness captures only part of the observable properties of generated code. We present CodeAssay, a taxonomy-first benchmark of 185 Python tasks across ten software-engineering categories. It combines audited ground truth, public tests for generation and repair, hidden tests for grading, mutation-based test-suite validation, and selected code-property measures. Regrading fixed model outputs after the audit changed 170 of 1,890 correctness labels (9.0%) and increased the measured best-to-worst model spread from 11.9 to 23.7 percentage points, although aggregate correctness remained nearly unchanged. The complete and hidden test suites achieved mutation scores of 82.6% and 74.8%, respectively. Across seven proprietary LLMs, standard-prompt correctness ranged from 77.3% to 98.9%, with significant differences in 12 of 21 model pairs. On the 120 tasks solved by all 14 model-prompt configurations, no model performed best across all selected code properties. A security-focused prompt produced no significant change in correctness or consistent reduction in the selected static-analysis findings, while increasing program length and cyclomatic complexity across all models. These findings show that reliable evaluation of LLM-generated code requires validated ground truth, protected tests, and multiple explicitly interpreted measures. CodeAssay provides a reproducible basis for evidence-based model evaluation in AI-augmented software development.

</details>


### [250] [EffiHolmes: Differential Profiling-Guided Repository Level Time Inefficiency Fix Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03558)
*Haowen Yang,Yun Peng,Zishuo Ding*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出EffiHolmes，一个基于LLM的仓库级时间效率缺陷定位框架，通过差分性能剖析、紧凑执行路径提取和领域引导的LLM推理来解决效率缺陷定位难题，并引入了首个仓库级效率缺陷定位基准RepoEffi-Bench。实验表明EffiHolmes在多个基线上取得显著改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型软件系统常存在时间效率问题（功能正确但执行时间过长），但其修复位置难以定位，因为效率缺陷不像功能缺陷那样产生测试失败或堆栈线索，传统和基于LLM的缺陷定位方法均不适用。运行时性能剖析提供了替代性证据，但在仓库级场景面临三个挑战：单次运行的剖析无法可靠区分效率热点与执行噪声；现有剖析器难以从大量后台执行中提取相关的执行路径；观测到的热点与实际修复位置之间存在语义鸿沟。

Method: EffiHolmes采用三阶段方法：（1）在默认和扩展工作负载下进行差分性能剖析，识别效率热点；（2）提取连接热点与报告的低效函数的紧凑执行路径；（3）利用领域引导的LLM推理定位底层低效逻辑。同时构建了RepoEffi-Bench，包含140个来自流行Python仓库的高质量问题作为基准。

Result: 实验结果显示EffiHolmes在文件级别Acc@3上以GPT-5.1提升4.29个百分点，在函数级别Acc@5上以qwen3-4b提升15.00个百分点，显著优于基于检索、智能体和剖析的SOTA基线，且在不同模型容量下均表现稳健。

Conclusion: EffiHolmes通过差分剖析与LLM推理结合的方式，有效解决了仓库级时间效率缺陷定位的难题，并通过RepoEffi-Bench基准验证了其有效性，展示了跨模型容量的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Large software systems often suffer from time inefficiencies that cause excessive execution time despite functional correctness. Localizing their fix locations is difficult because, unlike functional bugs, they produce neither test failures nor stack-trace clues, making traditional and recent LLM-based fault localization methods unsuitable. Runtime profiling provides alternative evidence but faces three challenges in repository-level settings: single-run profiling cannot reliably distinguish inefficiency hotspots from execution noise; existing profilers struggle to extract relevant execution paths from extensive background execution; and a semantic gap remains between observed hotspots and actual fix locations. We propose EffiHolmes, an LLM-based framework for repository-level time inefficiency fix localization. EffiHolmes uses differential profiling under default and scaled workloads to identify inefficiency hotspots, extracts compact execution paths connecting these hotspots to the reported inefficient function, and employs domain-guided LLM reasoning to locate the underlying inefficiency logic. We also introduce RepoEffi-Bench, the first benchmark for repository-level inefficiency localization, containing 140 high-quality issues collected from popular Python repositories. Experiments show that EffiHolmes consistently outperforms state-of-the-art retrieval-, agent-, and profiling-based baselines, improving file-level Acc@3 by 4.29 percentage points with GPT-5.1 and function-level Acc@5 by 15.00 percentage points with qwen3-4b. It also remains robust across model capacities.

</details>


### [251] [From Bug Reports to Browser-Executable Procedures: An LLM-Driven Agent for Web GUI Bug Reproduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03598)
*Cunming Zhang,Yu Pei,Michail Papadakis*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: ReBug是一个上下文感知的智能代理系统，能够从自然语言Web GUI bug报告中重建、执行并验证浏览器级别的复现流程，通过真实浏览器驱动实现端到端可视化执行和验证。在667个真实bug报告上，平均RSR为49.96%，任务完成率为74.96%，动作执行成功率为86.54%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Web GUI bug报告往往缺乏依赖和输入文件等前置条件，导致从自然语言报告复现bug非常困难。现有bug复现技术主要针对代码单元或移动应用，缺乏对Web GUI的端到端可视化执行和验证能力。

Method: ReBug将复现分为两个阶段：准备阶段从报告中重建缺失的前置条件并生成高层复现计划；执行阶段通过工具中介化的浏览器交互，维护页面状态和动作历史的结构化摘要，并根据报告推导的期望验证最终状态。

Result: 在来自四个开源Web应用的667个真实bug报告上，ReBug在受控当前部署中优于两个基线，平均RSR为49.96%，平均任务完成率为74.96%，平均动作执行成功率为86.54%。历史回放显示成功的流程通常能在恢复的buggy版本上暴露原有的bug行为。

Conclusion: 显式的上下文重建和状态感知的浏览器执行能有效支持从报告推导的浏览器复现，说明该方法在Web GUI bug复现领域具有实用价值。

Abstract: Reproducing web GUI bugs from natural-language bug reports is critical for software maintenance, but remains difficult because reports often lack prerequisites such as dependencies and input files. Existing bug reproduction techniques mainly target code units or mobile applications and lack end-to-end visual execution and validation for web GUIs. We present ReBug, a context-aware agent system that reconstructs, executes, and validates browser-level reproduction procedures from web GUI bug reports by driving a real browser. ReBug separates reproduction into two stages. In the preparation stage, ReBug reconstructs missing prerequisites from the report and available artifacts, and it produces a high-level reproduction plan. In the execution stage, it performs tool-mediated interactions in the browser, maintains structured summaries of page state and action history, and validates the final state against expectations derived from the report. We evaluate ReBug on 667 real-world bug reports from four open-source web applications. On controlled current deployments, ReBug outperforms both baselines, achieving an average RSR of 49.96%, a mean task completion rate of 74.96%, and a mean action execution success rate of 86.54%. Our results show that explicit context reconstruction and state-aware browser execution effectively support report-derived browser reproduction, while historical replay shows that successful procedures often expose the original bug-present behavior on restored buggy versions.

</details>


### [252] [Pattern over Pixels: Measuring Pattern Completion Bias in Multimodal Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03691)
*Khai-Nguyen Nguyen,Oscar Chaparro,Antonio Mastropaolo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文首次提出视觉模式补齐偏差基准测试，证明多模态大语言模型在将网页截图翻译为前端代码时，会因重复的UI模式而产生严重的偏差——即使模型能够识别异常元素，仍可能被模式一致性的答案所覆盖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）越来越多地用于将网页截图转换为前端代码，但重复的UI模式可能会诱使模型产生视觉上不正确但模式一致的输出。作者希望系统地测试这种重复网页模式如何损害MLLM在客观截图到代码填空任务上的准确性。

Method: 作者构建了首个视觉模式补齐偏差基准测试：在重复UI模式中扰动一个局部元素，模型需从截图和HTML上下文中恢复被掩蔽的宽度或字体大小值。从Design2Code数据集的30个网页出发，构建了1,440张评估截图，涵盖结构卡片和文本样式模式，并在标准条件和噪声叠加条件下进行评估，测试五个前沿MLLM。

Result: 所有模型都强烈偏向重复基线模式。卡片宽度扰动的平均偏差率达69.78%，文本字体大小扰动的平均偏差率达80.22%；而平均准确率分别仅为21.17%和7.89%。Codex-5.3表现最佳但仍从卡片的68.61%准确率降至文本的13.89%，Flash-3.0在文本上的偏差率高达96.11%。噪声、更细微的扰动和边界位置都会进一步增加偏差率。推理分析表明，更多的推理努力与更低的偏差相关，但模型即使识别出异常元素仍可能用模式一致的答案覆盖它。

Conclusion: 研究结果揭示了多模态代码生成中的具体失败模式，并表明其严重性与视觉显著性密切相关。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) are increasingly used to translate webpage screenshots into front-end code, but repeated UI patterns may sway them toward visually incorrect yet pattern-consistent outputs. In this work, we test how repeated webpage patterns hurt MLLM accuracy on an objective screenshot-to-code fill-in-the-blank task. We introduce the first benchmark for visual pattern-completion bias, where one localized element in a repeated UI pattern is perturbed and the model must recover the masked width or font-size value from the screenshot and HTML context. Starting from 30 webpages curated from the Design2Code dataset, we build 1,440 evaluated screenshots spanning structural card and text-style patterns under standard and noise-overlaid conditions. We evaluate five frontier MLLMs and find that all are strongly biased toward the repeated baseline. Mean bias rate reaches 69.78% on card-width perturbations and 80.22% on text font-size perturbations, while mean accuracy is only 21.17% and 7.89%, respectively. Codex-5.3 performs best but still drops from 68.61% accuracy on cards to 13.89% on text, while Flash-3.0 reaches 96.11% bias on text. Noise, subtler perturbations, and boundary positions further increase bias rate. Reasoning analysis further shows that greater reasoning effort correlates with lower bias, yet qualitative evidence reveals that models can identify the anomalous element and still override it with the pattern-consistent answer. Our results identify a concrete failure mode in multimodal code generation and show that its severity is strongly associated with visual saliency

</details>


### [253] [We Must Have Missed This Comment: Detecting and Repairing Stale Function References in Linux Kernel Comments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03734)
*Kexin Sun,Yunbo Lyu,Xutong Ma,Hongyu Kuang,Ratnadira Widyasari,He Zhang,Xiaoxing Ma,Julia Lawall,David Lo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: ReCite是一个三阶段方法，用于检测和修复Linux内核中因函数重构或删除而过时的代码注释引用。该方法通过检测未解析的函数形式符号、追踪Git历史演变、并基于LLM生成修复建议，在Linux内核v6.18-rc1上检测到869个过时引用，其中89%的修复建议有用，50个补丁已被接受。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Linux内核代码演进过程中，注释中引用的函数可能被重构或删除，导致注释变得过时。此类过时引用会误导维护者并阻碍代码理解。现有研究主要聚焦于Javadoc注释与直接标注函数之间的语义不一致，无法处理这种外部引发的过时问题，因此需要新的方法。

Method: ReCite采用三阶段方法：(1) 检测未解析的函数形式符号——识别注释中看似引用函数但在当前代码库中无法找到匹配函数的符号；(2) 通过Git历史追踪每个未解析符号的演变历史；(3) 基于演变历史和当前代码上下文，使用LLM生成修复建议。

Result: 在Linux内核v6.18-rc1上，ReCite检测到869个过时引用并生成修复建议。人工评估200个样本显示178个（89.0%）提供有用修复指导，85个（42.5%）可直接应用。提交的75个补丁中有50个已被接受。

Conclusion: ReCite有效地解决了Linux内核中因函数外部变化导致的注释过时引用问题，展示了较高的修复建议准确性和实际应用价值，同时对所有未解析的函数形式符号进行了实证研究。

Abstract: As the Linux kernel evolves, code comments may become outdated, as the functions they reference can be refactored or removed independently without corresponding updates to the comments. Such stale function references can mislead maintainers and thus hinder code comprehension. Prior work on detecting code-comment inconsistency mainly focused on addressing semantic misalignment between Javadoc comments and their directly annotated functions, making them inapplicable to this type of externally induced staleness in the Linux kernel. Therefore, we propose ReCite, a three-stage approach to identify and repair such stale references: (1) detecting unresolved function-form symbols -- symbols in comments that appear to reference functions but for which no matching function can be found in the current codebase, (2) tracing the evolution history of each unresolved symbol through the Git history, and (3) generating LLM-based repair suggestions grounded in the evolution history and current code context. On Linux kernel v6.18-rc1, ReCite detects 869 stale references with generated repair suggestions. A manual evaluation on 200 sampled repairs shows that 178 (89.0%) provide useful repair guidance, with 85 (42.5%) directly applicable. Of our 75 submitted patches, 50 have been accepted. We also empirically study all unresolved function-form symbols.

</details>


### [254] [Can LLMs Test Terminal User Interfaces?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03743)
*Chao Peng,Ruida Hu,Ajitha Rajan,Tegawendé F Bissyandé,Jacques Klein,Cuiyun Gao*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文调查了197个真实TUI应用，发现仅有12%的测试代码实际测试界面，45%的界面测试从不发送输入。作者构建了一个跨4个框架（ratatui/Rust、bubbletea/Go、textual/Python、ink/TypeScript）的无头基准测试，并比较了四个前沿LLM与随机探索的测试效果。结果表明：没有模型完全占优；随机探索在高吞吐量下崩溃发现优势明显，但LLM在单次交互层面更高效且能触达输入门控的缺陷；自动推导启动输入带来的收益最大；行覆盖率无法有效预测崩溃发现。同时发布了tuicov和tuibot两个工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TUI（终端用户界面）在现代开发者工具中已被广泛使用，兼具GUI的状态化、面向屏幕行为和终端部署特性，但缺乏专门的测试方法论。现有的测试实践无法有效验证TUI界面行为，这是一个亟待解决的研究空白。

Method: 作者首先对197个真实TUI应用的测试代码进行调查统计，然后构建了一个无头基准测试框架，将应用打包为带插桩的Docker镜像，记录行/组件覆盖率、渲染终端状态和崩溃。在相同时间预算下，对比四个前沿LLM与随机探索策略的测试效果，并测试自动推导启动输入策略的实际增益。

Result: 1) 没有LLM在所有指标上占优；2) 随机探索在时间预算限制下凭借更高吞吐量在崩溃发现上占优势，但按交互粒度计算，LLM引导效率更高且能发现随机无法触达的输入门控缺陷；3) 自动推导启动输入带来最大的实际收益，使原本无法启动的应用也能被测试；4) 行覆盖率对崩溃发现的预测能力很弱，不宜作为测试有效性的代理指标。

Conclusion: 自动化TUI测试是可行的但远未解决。研究强调了诚实基线（如随机探索）的重要性，其价值不亚于模型选择本身。该工作为TUI测试提供了基础设施工具（tuicov、tuibot），并指出了该领域需进一步研究的方向。

Abstract: Terminal User Interfaces (TUIs) combine the stateful, screen-oriented behaviour of GUIs with terminal deployment and are now common in developer tools. Yet they lack a dedicated testing methodology. We survey 197 real-world TUI applications: only 12% of test code exercises the interface, and 45% of those tests never send input, checking a static frame instead. We turn these applications into a headless benchmark spanning ratatui/Rust, bubbletea/Go, textual/Python, and ink/TypeScript, packaging each as an instrumented Docker image. We record line and widget coverage where reliable, rendered terminal states, and crashes. Under equal wall-clock budgets, we compare four frontier LLMs with random exploration. No model dominates. Random is a strong time-budgeted baseline, but its crash advantage comes from higher throughput: per interaction, LLM guidance is more efficient and uniquely reaches input-gated faults. Automatically deriving launch inputs yields the largest practical gain, enabling applications that otherwise never start. Line coverage poorly predicts crash discovery, weakening it as a proxy for test effectiveness. Automated TUI testing is feasible but far from solved, and honest baselines matter more than model choice. We release the coverage tool tuicov at https://github.com/tui-testing/tuicov and the testing framework tuibot at https://github.com/tui-testing/tuibot.

</details>


<div id='cs.SI'></div>

# cs.SI [[Back]](#toc)

### [255] [A Note on Reinforcement Learning to Develop Self-defined Agents' Behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03445)
*Matteo Morini,Pietro Terna*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种称为"交叉目标"(Cross Targets)的无监督学习技术，让智能体通过将动作猜测与结果猜测交叉训练来实现行为的内在一致性，从而在有限理性假设下自主发展简单行为策略。在图中随机游走者通过该"盲"策略学习，无需标签即可解决节点分类问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在有限理性（bounded rationality）假设下，智能体不应依赖外部监督信号或全局知识来学习行为策略，而是应当通过自身经验和简单的学习机制自发发展行为策略。本文探索如何让人工智能体通过无监督学习实现行为的内在一致性，并意外地发现这种简单方法能解决图上的节点分类问题。

Method: 提出Cross Targets（CT）学习技术：将智能体对"将要采取的动作"的预测数据与对"后续结果"的预测数据进行交叉使�用，作为训练数据来训练学习代理。这种"盲"策略训练不需要外部标签，仅依赖智能体自身的预测和实际结果之间的交叉组合。

Result: 实验表明，随机游走者（random walkers）仅通过学习如何停留在图中的同质区域，就能够解决节点分类问题，且无需任何明确的监督信号。这一结果令人意外，作者将其主要归因于观察者解释效应（observer interpretation effects）。

Conclusion: 简单的、无监督的、自发展行为策略可以产生解决复杂任务的涌现能力，这与有限理性假设一致。研究结果表明，简单的行为一致性训练可以产生超出预期的能力，同时强调了结果解读在评估智能体能力时的重要作用。

Abstract: The key point in this note is the self-development of simple behavior strategies, consistently with the bounded rationality hypothesis. Our artificial agents adopt learning techniques, mainly unsupervised, to achieve internal consistency in their behavior, with unexpected results. Those results can be considered mainly as the effects of the observer interpretation. The first technique in use has the name Cross Targets: to train the learning agent we use data crossed between the guesses about the action to be done and the guesses about the following results. An application of the CT "blind" strategy development is then presented: random walkers solve a node classification problem on a graph, after having learnt how to remain in homogeneous regions.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [256] [ISEE: Interactive Semantic Enrichment for Database Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02604)
*Yuan Tian,Yiru Chen,Rakesh R. Menon,Zifan Liu,Ting Cai,Fei Wu,Anudeep Chimakurthi,Prashanthi Ramamurthy,Sridevi Aishwariya Ganesan,Kun Qian,Yunyao Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个名为ISEE的交互式语义增强系统，针对LLM智能体在数据任务中因字段描述模糊或不完整导致的性能下降问题，通过评分系统量化描述质量、收集领域知识并与用户协作进行语义丰富，从而提升下游任务（如实体链接）的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体在数据相关任务中的性能高度依赖数据语义的清晰度和完整性，但实际中许多字段描述模糊或不完整，关键的领域上下文（如自定义字段的含义）通常来自用户的领域知识且很少公开记录，这严重限制了智能体在下游任务（如实体链接）中的表现。

Method: ISEE系统包含三个核心组件：(1) 评分系统，用于衡量数据字段描述的质量；(2) 领域知识收集模块，用于获取用户的领域背景信息；(3) 协作式语义增强流程，与用户共同完善字段描述。通过用户研究和自动化用户模拟进行评估。

Result: 通过用户研究、自动化用户模拟、定量评估和案例研究，结果表明ISEE显著降低了用户的认知负荷，提升了描述质量，并增强了下游任务（如实体链接）的性能。

Conclusion: ISEE系统有效弥合了数据语义缺失与LLM智能体需求之间的鸿沟，通过交互式语义增强机制为用户提供低认知负担的描述完善方案，从而提升LLM智能体在数据任务中的整体表现。

Abstract: LLM-based agents are increasingly being deployed for data-related tasks, including data sense-making, exploration, and retrieval. However, their performance heavily depends on the clarity and completeness of data semantics. In practice, many field descriptions remain ambiguous or incomplete, as much of the essential context (e.g., the meaning of a customized field) originates from users' domain knowledge and is rarely documented publicly. This gap restricts the agents' task performance in downstream tasks, such as entity-linking. To bridge this gap, we introduce a novel and comprehensive Interactive SEmantic Enrichment system (ISEE). Given a data field description, ISEE measures its quality through a scoring system, gathers domain knowledge, and collaboratively enriches the semantics with users. Through a user study, automated user simulation, quantitative evaluation, and case study, we demonstrate that ISEE significantly reduces cognitive load, improves description quality, and enhances downstream task performance.

</details>


### [257] [Self-Organising Digital Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02606)
*Marcello Barylli,Gabriel Béna,Alexander Mordvintsev,Eleni Nisioti,Sebastian Risi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出自组织数字电路（Self-Organising Digital Circuits），将电路逻辑生成与维护建模为图上的元学习问题，采用拓扑掩码Transformer配置布尔门的查找表（LUT），从零自组装功能电路，并能快速绕过永久性硬件故障和从软错误中实现接近完美恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统容错计算依赖静态策略（如硬件冗余和纠错码），而生物系统展现出适应性可塑性，能在损伤后通过动态重组维持功能。作者希望将生物学自组织原理引入数字硬件的容错设计中，以克服静态策略在应对不可预见故障时的局限性。

Method: 将功能逻辑生成与维护形式化为图上的元学习问题；使用拓扑掩码（topology-masked）Transformer来配置电路中布尔门的查找表；扩展神经细胞自动机（NCA）的模式生成范式，不是再生固定目标状态，而是在退化（degenerate）的布尔搜索空间中导航以满足计算任务。

Result: 该方法能从零自组装功能电路；能快速绕道处理永久的、未曾见过的硬件故障；对于软错误，策略在远超训练条件的损伤规模下达到>99.99%的接近完美恢复精度；在比训练时更宽的图上观察到跨电路规模泛化，准确率反而有所提升。

Conclusion: 该工作弥合了生物自组织原理与数字硬件实践之间的鸿沟，展示了将适应性可塑性引入硬件容错设计的新路径，为下一代自适应计算硬件提供了潜在范式。

Abstract: Fault tolerance in classical computing has traditionally relied on static strategies like hardware redundancy and error-correcting codes. Biological systems, in contrast, exhibit adaptive plasticity, maintaining function through dynamic re-organisation around damage. Inspired by this principle, we introduce Self-Organising Digital Circuits, framing functional logic generation and maintenance as a meta-learning problem on graphs. Our architecture employs a topology-masked Transformer that configures the Lookup Tables (LUT) of a circuit's Boolean gates. Extending the pattern-generation paradigm of Neural Cellular Automata (NCA), it navigates the degenerate Boolean search space to satisfy a computational task, rather than regenerating a fixed target state. We demonstrate that it can self-assemble functional circuits from scratch and rapidly re-route logic around permanent, previously unseen hardware faults. For soft errors, the policy achieves near-perfect recovery (>99.99\% accuracy) from damage sizes far exceeding training conditions. We further observe generalisation across circuit scales: accuracy improves on graphs substantially wider than those seen during training. This work bridges the principles of biological self-organisation with the practical domain of digital hardware.

</details>


### [258] [Beyond the Hivemind: Escaping LLM Homogeneity via Meta-Persona Anchoring and Sequential Temperature Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02618)
*Tairan Fu,Javier Conde,Carlos Arriaga,Gonzalo Martínez,Pedro Reviriego,Javier Coronado-Blázquez*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Meta-Persona Anchoring结合Filtered Temperature Scaling (FTS)的框架，用于缓解大语言模型中的"人工蜂群思维"效应——即模型在开放问题上收敛到狭窄同质化共识的现象。该框架通过两阶段生成过程（先自我选择独特个性锚定起点，再应用双重采样筛选）将平均成对余弦相似度从约0.85降至约0.65，显著提高了AI输出的多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在开放问题上表现出"人工蜂群思维"效应，导致语义坍塌，即使在高温度采样下，模型间响应的相似度仍然极高（约0.80-0.90），这限制了AI的多样性，使其难以接近人类层面的类型学多样性。

Method: 提出两阶段生成框架：(1) Meta-Persona Anchoring：提示模型自我选择独特、特质的个性作为生成起点；(2) Filtered Temperature Scaling (FTS)：采用双重阶段采样筛，先用Top-p过滤保证语法有效性，再在幸存候选中应用极端温度缩放（T≥4.0）以探索更广泛化的概率分布。

Result: 在INFINITY-CHAT数据集上对约20B参数的开源模型进行评估，平均成对余弦相似度从约0.85降至约0.65，大多数问题低于0.7的阈值，有效缩小了人工模式坍塌与人类级类型学多样性之间的差距。

Conclusion: 该方法显著降低了语义收敛性，提升了AI输出的多样性，并以开源框架形式发布，以支持更多样化、更具创造性的AI部署。

Abstract: Recent studies have identified an ``Artificial Hivemind'' effect in Large Language Models (LLMs) causing models to converge on a narrow, homogenized consensus even for open questions. This semantic collapse limits the diversity of AI, resulting in high inter-response similarity ($\approx 0.80-0.90$) even under high-temperature sampling. In this paper, we propose a novel mitigation framework to increase diversity: Meta-Persona Anchoring combined with Filtered Temperature Scaling (FTS). Our approach utilizes a two-stage generation process: first, the model is prompted to self-select a unique, idiosyncratic persona to anchor its starting point; second, we apply a dual-stage sampling sieve, utilizing Top-$p$ filtering to preserve grammatical validity followed by extreme temperature scaling ($T \ge 4.0$) on the surviving candidates to explore the broadened probability distribution. We evaluate our method using the INFINITY-CHAT dataset on state-of-the-art open weight models under $\sim$20B parameters. Our results demonstrate a significant reduction in semantic convergence, with average pairwise cosine similarity dropping from ($\approx 0.85$) to ($\approx 0.65$). Our scheme achieves a majority of questions below the 0.7 threshold, effectively reducing the gap between artificial mode collapse and human-level typological diversity. We provide our implementation as an open-source framework to enable more diverse and creative AI deployments.

</details>


### [259] [PULSE: An Executable Contract Language for Spatiotemporal Knowledge Graph Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02630)
*Dongxu Yang,Ziyi Liang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PULSE是一种受对象过程方法论(OPM)启发的语言，将知识图谱工程中的四个操作角色及其写效果局部化到一个类型化运行时中，提供效果限定引理和六项安全属性，并通过Lean 4形式化验证及大规模测试（88个测试、3,534次有界检查、37,440条时间轨迹）证明了与现有工作流的轨迹一致性和突变检测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 知识图谱工程中，状态的接受、观测、约束、过程和假设场景分散在不同工件中，其组合执行契约是外部的，导致状态管理缺乏统一性、安全性和可验证性。现有方法缺少对证据不可覆盖、分支隔离、计时器、状态变更守卫和事件排序等操作语义的形式化保障。

Method: 提出PULSE语言：采用OPM思想，将四种操作角色（mode）及其写效果定位在一个类型化运行时中。核心演算提供效果限定引理和六项安全属性。实现关键特性包括：证据不可覆盖、分支隔离、接地多主体计时器、守卫状态变更、声明排序的事件时空排序。外部运行器决定证据是否成为权威动作。GeoSPARQL、SOSA和SHACL作为生成视图。使用Lean 4验证位置、证据、时钟、监视器、原子性和分支源保留的内核类比。

Result: 88个测试、3,534次有界检查和32个Lean/Python运行时-内核用例验证实现。在冷链轨迹上重现了标准组合和Sismic状态图的轨迹。在37,440条生成的时间轨迹上与独立工作流匹配并区分十个单字段突变体。在NOAA IBTrACS 1980年以来完整子集上与GEOS和事件扫描在1,476,290个转换区对上一致（包括4,800个采样和12,831个持续限定事件）。GeoSPARQL探针测量接口覆盖率。

Conclusion: 研究结果支持合同局部化、安全论证和测试片段的轨迹一致性；但语言优越性和可用性不在评估范围内。表明PULSE在知识图谱工程中能提供形式化保证的同时保持与现有标准的兼容性。

Abstract: Knowledge graph engineering often distributes accepted state, observations, constraints, processes, and hypothetical scenarios across artifacts whose combined execution contract remains external. We present PULSE, an Object-Process-Methodology-inspired language that localizes four operational roles and their write effects in one typed runtime. Here, modes denote operational roles rather than modal or deontic logic. The implemented contract fixes evidence non-overwrite, branch isolation, grounded multi-subject timers, guarded state change, and declaration-ranked event ordering over time and space; an external runner still decides whether evidence becomes an authoritative move. GeoSPARQL, SOSA, and SHACL remain generated views. A core calculus gives an effect-confinement lemma and six safety properties. Lean 4 checks kernel analogues for positions, evidence, clocks, monitors, atomicity, and branch source retention; 88 tests, 3,534 bounded checks, and 32 Lean/Python runtime-kernel cases bound the implementation claim to the checked cases. First-author implementations of a standards composition and a separate Sismic statechart reproduce the tested cold-chain trace. Across 37,440 generated temporal traces, PULSE matches a separate workflow and distinguishes ten single-field mutants. On the complete NOAA IBTrACS since1980 subset it agrees with GEOS and an event sweep on 1,476,290 transition-zone pairs, including 4,800 sampled and 12,831 duration-qualified events. Project-specific GeoSPARQL probes measure interface coverage. Overall, the results support contract localization, safety arguments, and trace parity for the tested fragment; language superiority and usability remain outside the evaluation.

</details>


### [260] [HyperAgent: Planning and Acting over Tool-Schema Hypergraphs for Tool-Use LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02650)
*Zian Zhai,Xingyu Tan,Gaowang Zou,Xiaoyang Wang,Wenjie Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出HyperAgent框架，通过构建工具-模式超图（Tool-Schema Hypergraph）来建模工具间的关系，实现动态规划和执行，在AppWorld基准上提升了任务完成率，同时减少了冗余API调用和token消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的工具使用智能体通常依赖LLM从文本描述中推断工具组合，在复杂真实世界任务中会导致低效探索和不可靠执行。作者认为需要从模式（schema）层面构建结构化的工具关系模型，以克服隐式推理的局限性并适应真实执行环境的动态变化。

Method: 1) 在模式层面建模工具关系，构建有向工具-模式超图（Tool-Schema Hypergraph），工具表示为从输入模式节点到输出模式节点的超边。2) 设计HyperAgent框架：首先提取与任务相关的工具上下文图，指导构建模式感知的任务DAG；执行时通过缺陷导向扩展（deficit-oriented expansion）动态构建状态条件的工具支持图，识别未解决需求并根据当前智能体状态检索支撑工具。

Result: 在AppWorld基准上的实验表明，HyperAgent相比现有智能体基线，提升了任务完成性能，同时减少了冗余API调用、LLM交互次数和token消耗。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模工具间的模式级关系并利用超图结构指导动态规划与执行，HyperAgent能够更高效可靠地完成复杂工具使用任务，验证了结构化工具关系建模在LLM智能体中的有效性。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents increasingly rely on external tools to complete complex real-world tasks. However, reliable tool-use planning remains challenging due to the limitations of implicit reasoning and the evolving nature of real-world execution environments. Existing tool-use agents typically rely on LLMs to infer tool compositions from textual descriptions, which can lead to inefficient exploration and unreliable execution in complex tasks. To address these challenges, we model tool relations at the schema level and construct a directed Tool--Schema Hypergraph, in which tools are represented as hyperedges from their required input-schema nodes to their output-schema nodes. Furthermore, we propose HyperAgent, a Tool--Schema Hypergraph-guided framework for dynamic planning and execution. Given a task, HyperAgent first extracts a task-relevant tool context graph and uses it to guide the construction of a schema-aware Task DAG. During execution, HyperAgent dynamically realizes each subtask by constructing a state-conditioned tool support graph through deficit-oriented expansion, which identifies unresolved requirements and retrieves supporting producer tools according to the current agent state. Experiments on AppWorld demonstrate that HyperAgent improves task completion performance while reducing redundant API calls, LLM interactions, and token consumption compared with existing agent baselines.

</details>


### [261] [Explainable AI for the EU Right to Explanation: A Systematic Review of the Law-XAI Translation Gap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02699)
*Benjamin Fresz,Elena Dubovitskaya,Marco F. Huber*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对可解释人工智能（XAI）与欧盟"解释权"（Right to Explanation）的交叉领域进行了系统性文献综述，重点关注GDPR第15(1)(h)条和AI法案第86条。从2643篇文献中筛选出57篇全文，仅19篇实现了法律与技术视角的实质性整合。论文识别出三个问题模式，并提出了"接收者/目的框架"（Addressee/Purpose Framework）和四阶段操作化蓝图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当算法影响贷款、招聘、医疗等关键决策时，欧盟法律赋予个人"解释权"，但XAI能否在实践层面真正满足这一权利仍不清楚。这直接影响个人质疑自动决策的能力，然而现有多学科研究对法律与技术整合的理解不足。

Method: 采用系统性文献综述方法：从2024年起（因AI法案最终版于2024年7月发布，第86条为后期新增）进行宽泛检索，从2643篇初始记录中筛选57篇全文进行深入审查，评估法律与技术视角的整合程度，并识别跨语料库的问题模式。

Result: 仅19篇论文展示了法律与技术视角的实质性整合。识别出三个问题模式：(1)多数论文误认GDPR法律基础；(2)很少涉及欧洲法院（CJEU）的Dun & Bradstreet判决；(3)解释形式（由接收者决定）与内容（由法律目的决定）的区分经常被混淆。据此提出Addressee/Purpose框架和六项开放研究问题。

Conclusion: 当前XAI研究尚不足以满足欧盟"解释权"的实践要求。若无进一步进展，"解释权"将沦为一种形式义务而缺乏技术上可实现的合规路径。论文呼吁加强跨学科整合，并通过四阶段蓝图推动从理论到实践的操作化。

Abstract: When algorithms make or influence consequential decisions---about loan eligibility, hiring, or healthcare---EU law grants affected individuals a Right to Explanation. Yet whether (and how) Explainable AI (XAI) can satisfy this right in practice remains poorly understood, with direct implications for individuals' ability to contest automated decisions that affect their lives. This paper presents a systematic literature review of XAI in the context of the EU Right to Explanation, with particular focus on Art. 15(1)(h) GDPR, Art. 86 AI Act (AIA), and related instruments. We consider papers published from 2024 onwards, as the final version of the AIA was published in July 2024---with Art. 86 being added late. From 2643 initial records identified by a deliberately broad search, we review 57 full texts, of which only 19 papers demonstrate substantive integration of both legal and technical perspectives, showing gaps in the interdisciplinary synthesis of the current regulatory framework. We document three problematic patterns across the corpus: Most misidentify the GDPR legal basis; few engage with the CJEU's Dun & Bradstreet judgment (likely due to publication timing); and the distinction between explanation form (governed by addressee) and content (governed by legal purpose) is often conflated. We conceptualize this as the Addressee/Purpose Framework, propose a four-phase blueprint for operationalization, and identify six concrete open research questions. Without further progress, the Right to Explanation risks remaining a formal obligation without a technically realizable path to compliance.

</details>


### [262] [Predictive Set Theory: A Generative Framework for Cognitive Architecture with Operationalized Core Mechanisms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02704)
*Yiyang Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出预测集合论（PST），一个从第一性原理重建认知架构的形式化生成框架，以三个最小操作（身份函数的传感器、集合论状态刷新、三种引用链）为基础，推导出状态序列、需求、比较、效率和有限视界概率规划等核心认知功能，并为罗素悖论、哥德尔不完备性、负反馈接地和电影剪辑理解等经典问题提供新解法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前的预测处理理论缺乏对"预测"结构的操作性定义、对预测误差的标准化响应以及跨连续更新的一致性维持机制的规范说明；而贝叶斯认知科学预设了封闭的假设空间，未能生成性地解释概率分布对象如何成为离散、可识别的指称物。两者均缺乏从第一性原理出发的生成性解释。

Method: PST将认知锚定在一组最小操作集上：将传感器形式化为恒等函数、集合论状态刷新、以及三种基本引用链（引用、反引用、半引用）。从这些基础构件严格推导出核心认知功能，包括状态序列、需求、比较、效率和有限视界概率规划。该框架不是模拟神经机制，而是作为任何在不完全信息和不可逆风险下需保持内部一致性的系统的设计规范。

Result: PST为经典问题提供了新颖的解决方案，包括罗素悖论、哥德尔不完备性的认知地位、负反馈的接地问题以及电影剪辑的理解机制。论文的主要目的是通过公开学术记录确立PST框架的原创性和完整性。

Conclusion: PST构成一个设计规范而非神经机制的描述，适用于任何需在信息不完备与不可逆风险条件下维持内部一致性并行动的系统。该框架的核心贡献在于以生成性方式重建认知架构，弥补了预测编码和贝叶斯方法在操作性定义与生成性解释方面的不足。

Abstract: Predictive processing theories portray the brain as a hierarchical prediction engine that minimizes prediction error, yet they lack operational definitions for the structure of a "prediction," the standardized response to a prediction error, and the mechanism that maintains consistency across successive updates. Bayesian cognitive science attempts to subsume all uncertainty under probabilistic belief updating, but it presupposes a closed hypothesis space and provides no generative account of how the objects over which probabilities are distributed become discrete, identifiable referents in the first place. This paper introduces Predictive Set Theory (PST), a formal generative framework that reconstructs cognitive architecture from first principles. PST anchors cognition in a minimal set of operations---a sensor formalized as an identity function, set-theoretic state refresh, and three fundamental forms of reference chains (reference, counter-reference, and semi-reference)---and rigorously derives core cognitive functions including state sequences, demand, comparison, efficiency, and finite-horizon probabilistic planning. Rather than modeling neural mechanisms, PST constitutes a design specification for any system that must maintain internal consistency while acting under incomplete information and irreversible risk. The framework offers novel resolutions to classical problems such as Russell's paradox, the cognitive status of Gödelian incompleteness, the grounding of negative feedback, and the comprehension of film editing. The primary purpose of this paper is to establish, through the public academic record, the originality and completeness of the Predictive Set Theory framework.

</details>


### [263] [Towards a new paradigm of scientific discovery with socialized artificial intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02775)
*Xinjie Yao,Xingxin Xu,Xiyuan Gao,Zhoupeng Guo,Kunlong Yang,Dengyu Zhao,Siqi Zhao,Zhihe Fan,Yichen Dong,Xin Li,Jiekang Feng,Jiahe Wu,Sen Wang,Beiming Yu,Kejia Zhao,Ruipu Zhao,Jiaqi Zhou,Heyang Li,Jianjun Chen,Anbo Dai,Xin Liu,Zhengtao Yu,Qinghua Hu,Pengfei Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出BLAZE（Bridging Literature, Agents, and Zero-gap Experimentation）范式，将AI从孤立研究任务的助手重新定位为科学发现的组织基础设施，通过连接知识、推理、实验验证和人类判断，构建持续的研究生命周期，使科学发现更加可追踪、可复现和累积性，实现"社会化科学智能"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学发现经历了观察实验、理论、计算和数据驱动等多次范式转变，当前核心挑战不再是生产更多信息，而是将不断扩展的知识、推理和证据组织成连贯的发现过程。现有AI系统仅作为孤立任务的辅助工具，缺乏系统性的整合能力。

Method: BLAZE将AI视为科学发现的组织基础设施，而非孤立任务的助手。它通过整合持久知识、集体推理、实证验证和人类判断，将碎片化的研究活动转化为累积性的探究、批判和修订过程，并组织人类与机器共享科学流程。

Result: BLAZE使科学发现更加可追踪、可复现和累积性，同时保留人类的创造力、判断力和责任，扩展了集体科学探究的规模、深度和连续性。

Conclusion: 社会化科学智能可能为科学的下一个时代奠定基础，其目的不是取代人类发现，而是扩展集体科学探究的规模、深度和连续性。

Abstract: Scientific discovery has advanced through successive transformations in the organization of knowledge. Observation and experimentation established the empirical foundations of science. Theory made it possible to derive general principles from particular phenomena. Computation extended inquiry into systems beyond direct observation, while data-intensive methods opened new spaces of pattern and prediction. Science now confronts a different frontier. The central challenge is no longer simply to produce more information, but to organize expanding knowledge, reasoning, and evidence into a coherent process of discovery. Here, we introduce Bridging Literature, Agents, and Zero-gap Experimentation (BLAZE), a paradigm of socialized scientific intelligence. BLAZE conceives AI not as an assistant for isolated research tasks, but as an organizational infrastructure for scientific discovery. It connects persistent knowledge, collective reasoning, empirical validation, and human judgment within a continuous research lifecycle, transforming fragmented activities into a cumulative process of inquiry, criticism, and revision. The central premise of BLAZE is that scientific intelligence does not arise from computation alone. It emerges from the sustained interaction among knowledge, hypotheses, experiments, and collective verification. By organizing humans and machines within a shared scientific process, BLAZE makes discovery more traceable, reproducible, and cumulative while preserving human creativity, judgment, and responsibility. Socialized scientific intelligence may provide a foundation for the next era of science. Its purpose is not to replace human discovery, but to extend the scale, depth, and continuity of collective scientific inquiry.

</details>


### [264] [BAP-SQL: Budget-Aware Observation Planning for Agentic Text-to-SQL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02876)
*Chong Peng,Pin Qian,Su Wang,Yihang Chen,Varun Sah*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BAP-SQL将观测形成视为预算控制阶段，通过估计查询风险、重写SQL以及独立的运行时防护盾来管理代理式文本到SQL任务中的上下文和数据库工作预算，在tight-budget设置下提升了成功率并减少了Token消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工具使用代理不只是消耗观测，其行动决定了后续观测内容。在代理式文本到SQL中，宽泛查询会浪费上下文和数据库工作量，而事后压缩无法恢复遗漏的行或已消耗的工作，因此需要在进行观测前就进行预算控制。

Method: BAP-SQL将观测形成作为一个预算控制阶段：估计查询风险、在有用时重写SQL、并将硬性限制委派给独立的运行时防护盾。

Result: 在4B通用模型、FINER-SQL 4B专用模型和7B骨干上均提升了tight-budget成功率。在主要BIRD衍生设置中，相比匹配的SFT提升了3.4/3.6个百分点，同时节省4.5/5.0%的Token。但收益随模型能力和预算增加而减弱，在最宽松设置下甚至反转，且未减少数据库工作量。

Conclusion: 匹配的再训练和任务级迁移表明收益源于策略可见的规划和预算敏感的救助机制。BAP-SQL在预算受限场景下有效，但其优势在预算和模型能力充裕时消失，提示预算控制应在适当条件下自适应应用。

Abstract: Tool-using agents do not merely consume observations: their actions determine what arrives next. In agentic text-to-SQL, a broad query can spend context and database work before useful evidence appears, while post-hoc compression cannot recover omitted rows or expended work. We present BAP-SQL, which treats observation formation as a budget-control stage: it estimates query risk, rewrites SQL when useful, and delegates hard limits to an independent runtime shield. Across general 4B, specialized FINER-SQL 4B, and 7B backbones, BAP-SQL improves tight-budget success. On the primary BIRD-derived setting, it gains 3.4/3.6 percentage points over matched SFT while using 4.5/5.0% fewer tokens. Matched retraining and task-level transfer associate the gain with policy-visible planning and budget-sensitive rescue. The benefit attenuates as model capability and budget increase, reverses at the loosest setting, and does not reduce database work.

</details>


### [265] [VeriTrace: Human-Like Temporal Exploration Completes Agentic Action Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02878)
*Yu-Tung Liu,Cunxi Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: VeriTrace是一个多智能体系统，通过赋予Inspector智能体完整的调试动作空间（独立控制信号选择、时间窗口和迭代深度），实现了Verilog RTL自动生成中首个100% Pass@1准确率，超越了现有系统约95%的性能上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有最先进的多智能体系统在Verilog RTL生成基准上约95%准确率处达到瓶颈，原因在于调试动作空间不完整——系统中的智能体在信号选择、可查询的时间窗口范围上受到限制，将调试降级为对电路行为的狭窄预定义视图进行模式匹配，而非基于假设驱动的根本原因分析。

Method: VeriTrace提出一种称为Agentic Temporal Exploration的多智能体系统方法。其Inspector智能体拥有完整的调试动作空间，能够独立控制信号选择、时间窗口边界和迭代深度。智能体可以形成关于故障原因的假设、查询波形获取证据，并迭代地细化理解，模拟人类验证工程师的探索性过程。

Result: VeriTrace在VerilogEval-V2基准上达到100%的Pass@1准确率，是首个在该基准上实现完美功能正确性的系统。在共享Claude Sonnet 4.0骨干模型下，VeriTrace比最强复现基线提升5.1个百分点。

Conclusion: 调试代理能力（debugging agency）弥合了最终的准确率差距，证明完整的调试动作空间和假设驱动的探索式调试是突破LLM驱动的硬件设计自动化性能瓶颈的关键。

Abstract: Large language models have shown promise for automated Verilog RTL generation, yet state-of-the-art multi-agent systems plateau at ~95% accuracy on standard benchmarks. We trace this ceiling to an incomplete debugging action space: existing systems restrict which signals the agent can inspect, which time windows it can query, or both, reducing debugging to pattern matching on a narrow, predetermined view of circuit behavior rather than hypothesis-driven root-cause analysis. We present VeriTrace, a multi-agent system whose Inspector agent operates over a complete debugging action space, with independent control over signal selection, time-window bounds, and iteration depth. This capability, which we term Agentic Temporal Exploration, enables the agent to form hypotheses about failure causes, query the waveform for evidence, and refine its understanding iteratively, mirroring the exploratory process of human verification engineers. VeriTrace achieves 100\% Pass@1 on VerilogEval-V2, the first system to attain perfect functional correctness on this benchmark. On a shared Claude Sonnet 4.0 backbone, VeriTrace outperforms the strongest reproduced baseline by +5.1%, demonstrating that debugging agency closes the final accuracy gap.

</details>


### [266] [Interpreting Black-Box Large Language Models with Sentence-Level Energy Landscapes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02879)
*Maryam Rezaee,Pooriya Safaei,Maryam Asgarinezhad,Fatemeh Seyyedsalehi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向闭源大语言模型（LLM）的模型无关、事后归因解释器。该方法训练一个基于能量的模型（EBM）作为代理，捕捉提示与响应之间的概念一致性，并引导轻量级解释器网络的训练。该解释器可独立运行，训练完成后无需再调用LLM API即可量化提示句子对目标输出的影响力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 商业闭源LLM只能通过封闭API访问，缺乏可解释性，给负责任部署带来关键挑战。现有可解释方法通常需要模型内部信息，无法适用于黑盒API场景。

Method: 1) 训练一个Energy-Based Model (EBM) 作为代理模型，建模提示与响应之间的概念一致性；2) 利用能量景观指导轻量级解释器网络的训练；3) 解释器在句子层面归因，训练完成后可独立工作，无需额外API查询；4) 通过跨多样输入的全局训练来捕捉更广泛的生成模式，缓解实例特定偏差。

Result: 实验表明EBM能准确模拟目标LLM，解释器能有效识别对生成特定目标输出最具影响力的提示句子。

Conclusion: 该框架成功实现了对闭源LLM的模型无关事后解释，在无API查询的情况下提供句子级归因，兼顾全局生成模式与局部可解释性。

Abstract: The widespread adoption of proprietary Large Language Models (LLMs) accessed strictly through closed APIs has created a critical challenge for responsible deployment: a fundamental lack of interpretability. To address this, we propose a model-agnostic, post-hoc attribution interpreter operating at the sentence level. Our approach trains an Energy-Based Model (EBM) as a surrogate to capture the LLM's internal conceptual consistency between prompts and responses. This energy landscape guides the training of a lightweight interpreter network. Uniquely, our interpreter operates as a standalone tool; once trained, it quantifies the influence of prompt sentences on a user-specified target output without requiring further API queries to the LLM. By globally training a local interpreter across diverse inputs, our framework captures broader generation patterns and mitigates instance-specific biases. Experiments demonstrate that our EBM accurately simulates the target LLM, allowing the interpreter to effectively identify the prompt sentences most influential in generating specific target outputs.

</details>


### [267] [Hypercubes, Hyperplanes, and Constraint-Induced Complexity Collapse in Atomic Concept Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02930)
*Irene Tsapara*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过超立方体与超平面的几何视角重新审视高元原子概念学习，发现实例空间的逻辑复杂度并非均匀分布，而是由超平面组织：除全对角线外的超平面在有限多个初等等价类中塌缩（且界限与项深度无关），而全对角线是例外情况，其类数无界增长。基于作者先前建立的高维框架，通过规范简单概念、极小序和代表性归约来解释这一不对称性，并给出了二元的完整工作案例和三元的显式处理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者观察到原子概念的r维超立方体在结构上并非均匀的，其逻辑复杂度由超平面组织，且存在显著的不对称性——全对角线在逻辑复杂度上与其他超平面有本质区别。这种几何不对称性反映了概念本身的归约理论结构，需要用几何-逻辑视角来阐明复杂度在原子概念学习中的集中位置。

Method: 从r维超立方体的几何结构出发，分析每个超平面的初等等价类结构；利用作者先前的高维框架（规范简单概念、极小序、代表性归约）重新解释这些几何结果；通过二元完全工作案例和三元超立方体的显式处理来展示一般性原理；最后以约束假设空间和结构化分类的现代视角进行诠释。

Result: 建立了高维中超平面行为的分类学，表明复杂度是局部化的而非均匀分布在实例空间中；三维案例展示了正交族、部分对角线和例外全对角线等本质现象；证明了除全对角线外所有超平面的等价类数量有与项深度无关的界限。

Conclusion: 复杂度在原子概念学习中集中于特定区域（全对角线），而非均匀分布；这种几何-逻辑视角为理解约束假设空间和结构化分类提供了新的现代诠释。

Abstract: We revisit higher-arity atomic concept learning through the geometry of hypercubes and hyperplanes of ground instances. Our starting point is the observation that the ambient r-dimensional hypercube of ground atoms is not structurally uniform. Its logical complexity is organized by hyperplanes: every hyperplane other than the full diagonal collapses into finitely many elementary-equivalence classes, with a bound independent of the term depth, while the full diagonal is exceptional and its class count grows without bound. This asymmetry is not merely geometric. It reflects the reduction-theoretic structure of the concepts themselves. Building on a higher-dimensional framework developed in the author's earlier work, we reinterpret these results through canonical simple concepts, minimal orderings, and representative reductions. This yields a taxonomy of hyperplane behavior in higher dimensions and shows that complexity is localized rather than spread uniformly through the instance space. The paper includes a fully worked binary case, an explicit treatment of the ternary hypercube, and an unpacked account of the reduction machinery that drives the collapse. The three-dimensional case already exhibits the essential phenomenon of orthogonal families, partial diagonals, and the exceptional full diagonal. This geometric-logical perspective clarifies where complexity is concentrated in atomic concept learning and suggests a modern interpretation in terms of constrained hypothesis spaces and structured classification.

</details>


### [268] [When Compression Scores Cannot Decide: Information Boundaries for Group-Robust LLM Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02940)
*Andrew Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出一个可复现的压缩统计量也可能选出错误候选，并通过信息接口理论建模了统计量选择与真实端点性能之间的差距。研究发现群解析对角恢复方法能较好恢复粗粒度损伤排序（Spearman 0.9239），粗粒度深度分配相比均衡均匀分配可降低最差群困惑度膨胀12.6-20.9%，模型特定的完整掩码端点选择进一步改善2.7-8.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 即使统计量具有高可复现性（split-half可靠性0.906），其预测的增益（16.1%）与实际选择的端点性能存在显著差距（比两个对照组差6.0%和7.7%）。这一现象表明需要更深入地理解统计量选择与真实损伤之间的信息边界。

Method: 通过信息接口（information interfaces）建模统计量支持的区分能力。提出了圆锥定律（conic law）精确给出等权重组下正线性固定候选损伤的池化代价。构建了三种两世界结构与精确观测纤维半径来刻画池化矩、组局部矩和参考路径曲率的未决问题。在多个稠密LLM和OLMoE上验证了粗粒度分配与模型特定端点选择策略。

Result: 群解析对角恢复方法在粗粒度损伤排序上取得Spearman 0.9239。粗粒度深度分配相比均衡均匀分配将最差困惑度膨胀降低12.6-20.9%。模型特定完整掩码端点选择相比参考方法改善2.7-8.0%。OLMoE路由器迹线预测单方向114/192（优于最强重标注下的81/192）。单层有限菜单决策带来保留最差组KL降低13.7%和7.2%。

Conclusion: 局部测量只能用于构建候选，选择权的合法性来自完整的候选端点或经过验证的均匀保证，且不确定性校准需覆盖每次比较。这为模型压缩中的统计量评估与端点选择提供了理论基础和实用指导。

Abstract: A reproducible compression statistic can still select the wrong candidate. A dense pruning score with 0.906 split-half reliability predicted a 16.1% gain. Its selected endpoint was 6.0% and 7.7% worse than two controls. We model the gap through information interfaces that delimit which distinctions each statistic supports. For equal-weight groups, a conic law gives the exact pooling price for positive linear fixed-candidate damage, including diagonal and full PSD second moments. Three two-world constructions and an exact observation-fiber radius characterize what pooled moments, group-local moments, and reference-path curvature leave unresolved. A group-resolved diagonal recovers broad damage order (Spearman 0.9239) while fine order remains weak. Relative to balanced uniform allocation, a coarse depth allocation cuts worst-group perplexity inflation by 12.6--20.9% across three dense LLMs. Model-specific complete-mask endpoint selection improves over those references by 2.7--8.0%. In OLMoE, router traces predict singleton direction (114/192 versus 81/192 under the strongest relabeling). Finite-menu decisions on one layer yield held-out worst-group KL reductions of 13.7% and 7.2%. Local measurements construct candidates. Selection is licensed by complete candidate endpoints or a validated uniform guarantee, with uncertainty calibrated to every comparison.

</details>


### [269] [On the missing data layer and a potential solution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02949)
*Francis F Daniel,Mauro Ibañez,Francis Perelman,Marian Basti*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DataHub，一个面向拉丁美洲AI数据集的基础设施，通过任务优先的Ontology（<task?>/<domain?>/<language?>）来解决数据集发现和供给两大问题，包括数据发现、元数据、贡献、许可和复用机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拉丁美洲缺乏AI基础设施的两个基础层：数据层和基准层。数据层面临两个复合问题：一是发现困难，拉丁美洲的AI数据集分散在各平台且没有共享索引；二是供给不足，即使有完美索引，现有数据总量仍远低于前沿AI开发所需。

Method: 提出DataHub，一种任务优先的数据基础设施，通过Ontology <task?>/<domain?>/<language?> 组织，并包含数据集发现、元数据、贡献、许可和复用等机制。

Result: 提出了一种针对拉丁美洲AI数据集层缺失问题的解决方案框架DataHub，但目前仅停留在提案层面，尚未给出实证结果。

Conclusion: DataHub通过任务优先的组织方式，旨在建立拉丁美洲AI数据集层的共享基础设施，以解决数据发现和供给的双重挑战。

Abstract: Latin America is missing two foundational layers of AI infrastructure: the dataset layer and the benchmark layer. This paper targets the dataset layer. The dataset layer faces two compounding problems: discovery and supply. Latin American AI datasets exist but are scattered across platforms with no shared index. Even with perfect indexing, the total volume would remain far below what frontier AI development requires. We propose DataHub: a task-first data infrastructure organized through the ontology /<task?>/<domain?>/<language?>, with mechanisms for dataset discovery, metadata, contribution, licensing, and reuse.

</details>


### [270] [Neurosymbolic Reasoning with Incremental Knowledge for Sample Efficient Hierarchical Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02993)
*Subrat Prasad Panda,Blaise Genest,Arvind Easwaran*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种融合符号规划与神经强化学习的神经符号分层强化学习框架（InK），通过增量知识更新的符号高层组件与低层目标条件神经模块相结合，在稀疏奖励的长视距导航任务中显著提升了样本效率，并提出了信念世界树搜索（BWTS）以利用先验知识进行最优符号规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统强化学习在稀疏奖励、长视距推理环境中面临严重的样本效率问题。现有分层强化学习（HRL）将知识以固定、不可更新的形式编码（如架构选择），无法在探索过程中利用逐步获得的增量知识进行推理，导致推理能力受限、样本效率低下。

Method: 提出神经符号分层强化学习框架InK：(1) 符号高层组件在可更新的增量知识表示上执行符号规划（如D*算法），实现灵活的推理；(2) 低层目标条件神经模块通过奖励塑形从经验中学习运动原语；(3) 提出信念世界树搜索（BWTS）方法，在给定世界先验知识的前提下执行最优符号规划。

Result: 在导航任务上的实验表明，引入增量知识（InK）显著提升了样本效率；同时，BWTS能够在带有先验知识的情况下实现最优符号规划。

Conclusion: 通过将增量更新的符号知识与低层神经策略相结合，神经符号分层强化学习能够有效克服固定HRL在探索过程中无法利用渐进知识的缺陷，显著改善稀疏奖励长视距任务中的样本效率，为知识驱动型强化学习提供了新思路。

Abstract: (Flat) Reinforcement Learning (RL) agents face significant challenges in environments with sparse rewards that require long-horizon reasoning. A compelling approach to improve sample efficiency is to incorporate knowledge into learning and decision-making. In standard Hierarchical RL (HRL), knowledge is encoded in a fixed, non-updatable form, such as architectural choices, and remains unchanged throughout learning. With fixed HRL, reasoning with incremental knowledge learned during exploration is impractical before sufficient environmental knowledge is acquired, leading to poor sample efficiency. In this work, we propose neurosymbolic HRL with {\em Incremental Knowledge (InK)}: symbolic high-level components perform {\em symbolic planning} (e.g. using $D^*$) on an updatable representation of current InK, while low-level goal-conditioned neural modules learn motion primitives through experience using reward shaping. Experiments on navigation tasks demonstrate that incorporating InK substantially improves sample efficiency. Additionally, to perform {\em optimal} symbolic planning given {\em prior} knowledge about the world, we develop Belief World Tree Search. The code is available at https://github.com/CPS-research-group/ink_bwts.

</details>


### [271] [On the missing benchmarks layer and a potential solution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02996)
*Francis F Daniel,Mauro Ibañez,Francis Perelman,Marian Basti*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出拉丁美洲缺少AI基准层（benchmark layer）这一问题，并提出了EvalsHub这一开放的任务优先基准基础设施，其首个区域实例为LatamBoard，旨在让大学、公共机构、专业社区和企业能够发布、执行、比较和维护AI评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拉丁美洲缺乏AI原生发展的基础层——基准层。基准层有两个不可替代的功能：一是审计AI系统是否符合区域社会需求，二是引导AI在相关经济环境中进行优化。没有基准层，公共机构无法独立评估外国AI系统，公司也无法以SOTA性能优化AI来解决本地问题。缺失该层的代价是双重的：可审计性的丧失和优化方向的缺失。

Method: 提出EvalsHub，一个开放、任务优先的基准基础设施，其首个区域实例为LatamBoard。该平台支持大学、公共机构、专业社区和公司发布、执行、比较和维护各种评估（覆盖模型、工作流和代理）。采用"一次构建、永久测量"的设计理念，机构可在新AI系统发布时重新运行评估，行业团队在系统变更后也可运行评估。平台设计上注重开放性和激励驱动性。

Result: 提出EvalsHub框架及LatamBoard区域实例的设计，但尚未展示实证结果，属于概念/基础设施层面的提案。

Conclusion: 构建EvalsHub和LatamBoard可以填补拉丁美洲AI基准层的空白，使该地区能够独立审计AI系统并对本地问题的优化方向进行引导，从而解决可审计性和优化方向的缺失问题。

Abstract: Latin America is missing a foundational layer for native AI development: the benchmark layer. The benchmark layer does two things no other layer can - it audits AI systems against regional social requirements and it directs AI optimization in economically relevant environments. Without it, public institutions cannot independently evaluate foreign AI systems, and companies cannot optimize AI systems to solve local problems with SOTA performance. The cost of the missing layer is dual: a loss of auditability and a loss of optimization direction over a technology that is increasingly critical infrastructure. We propose an EvalsHub, with LatamBoard as its first regional instance - an open, task-first benchmark infrastructure where universities, public institutions, professional communities, and companies can publish, execute, compare, and maintain evaluations across models, workflows, and agents. Built once, measured forever - re-run by institutions as new AI systems ship and by industry teams after every system change. Open by design and incentive-driven by construction.

</details>


### [272] [ProPRL: Property-Aware Prerequisite Relation Learning in Educational Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03006)
*Xinghe Cheng,Jiapu Wang,Chaobo He,Ruihai Dong,Quanlong Guan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ProPRL框架，通过从概念-资源超图和有向学习行为图学习互补概念表示，并结合Pair-conditioned Gate自适应融合双视图，以及Irreversibility Constraint约束反对称性，实现更优的先修关系学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有先修关系学习方法常将其视为传统链接预测任务，存在两个局限：一是无法针对每个候选概念对自适应整合互补的教育证据；二是无法抑制矛盾的逆向预测（即同一概念对被同时预测为两个方向的先修关系）。

Method: ProPRL包含三个关键组件：（1）从概念-资源超图和有向学习行为图学习互补的概念表示，其中方向保持的个性化传播聚合多跳行为证据；（2）Pair-conditioned Gate针对每个候选有序概念对自适应地对两个视图进行加权融合；（3）Irreversibility Constraint引入反对称正则化项，惩罚同一概念对在两个方向上同时具有高置信度的预测。

Result: 在多个真实世界教育数据集上的实验表明，ProPRL在先修关系学习任务上达到了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: 通过属性感知的多视图学习、对级自适应门控融合和反对称约束，ProPRL有效克服了传统链接预测方法的局限，提升先修关系学习的准确性与一致性。

Abstract: Prerequisite relation learning is central to adaptive instruction, yet existing methods often formulate it as conventional link prediction, limiting their ability to adaptively integrate complementary educational evidence for individual candidate pairs and to discourage contradictory reverse predictions. We propose ProPRL, a Property-aware Prerequisite Relation Learning framework. ProPRL first learns complementary concept representations from a concept-resource hypergraph and a directed learning-behavior graph, where direction-preserving personalized propagation aggregates multi-hop behavioral evidence. It then employs a Pair-conditioned Gate to adaptively weight and fuse the two views for each candidate ordered concept pair. Finally, an \textit{Irreversibility Constraint} introduces an anti-symmetry regularizer that penalizes simultaneously high confidence in both directions of the same concept pair. Experiments on multiple real-world educational datasets show that ProPRL achieves state-of-the-art performance on prerequisite relation learning.

</details>


### [273] [UrbanAgent: A Tool-Augmented Agent for Cross-System Urban Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03018)
*Jiayu Cao,Xingyuan Zeng,feiyu Li,Zhijing Huang,Xujie Yuan,Rongxiang Chen,Shimin Di,Libin Zheng,Jian Yin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Urban-Agent，一个面向跨系统城市任务的工具增强型智能体框架，将大语言模型的认知与推理能力与代码执行、API调用和Model Context Protocol等工具集结合，通过自适应闭环在行动前澄清缺失信息、基于实时观测使用工具、并使最终响应与观测证据和任务约束对齐。同时引入Urban-Eval基准，同时评估任务结果和执行质量（包括工具覆盖率、依赖有效性和证据可追溯性），实验显示Urban-Agent达到71%的任务成功率，比最强基线高出10个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代城市依赖大量碎片化、互操作性差的数字服务，现有数字平台、城市基础模型和智能助手只能解决城市任务的孤立方面，难以将复杂的自然语言请求可靠地转换为可执行的跨系统工作流，存在明显的评估缺口。

Method: Urban-Agent框架：将LLM的推理能力与工具集（代码执行、API调用、Model Context Protocol）耦合，通过单一自适应闭环实现信息澄清、实时观测驱动的工具使用、以及基于证据和任务约束的对齐。Urban-Eval基准：专门针对跨系统城市请求，同时评估任务结果和执行质量，包括工具覆盖率、依赖有效性和证据可追溯性。

Result: Urban-Agent在Urban-Eval基准上达到71%的任务成功率，比最强基线高出10个百分点，且在GPT-5-mini、Gemini-2.5-flash、DeepSeek-V4-flash和Qwen3-235B-A22B等多个模型上均保持领先优势。

Conclusion: Urban-Agent通过工具增强和自适应闭环有效解决了跨系统城市任务的复杂工作流生成问题，Urban-Eval提供了更全面的评估框架，同时涵盖任务结果与执行质量两个维度，为城市智能体领域设立了新的评估标准。

Abstract: Modern cities rely on an increasing number of digital services to operate, but residents' daily needs are still difficult to meet. Services are fragmented and have little interoperability, placing a heavy operational burden on users. Existing digital platforms, urban foundation models, and intelligent assistants each address only isolated aspects of an urban task. But they struggle to reliably convert complex natural-language requests into executable cross-system workflows. We propose Urban-Agent, a tool-augmented agent framework for cross-system urban tasks. It couples the cognitive and reasoning capabilities of a large language model with a tool-set supporting code execution, API calls, and Model Context Protocol. Through one adaptive closed loop, it clarifies missing information before acting, grounds tool use in live observations, and aligns the final response with observed evidence and task constraints. To address the evaluation gap, we introduce Urban-Eval, a benchmark specifically designed for cross-system urban request. Unlike prior benchmarks that assess either general tool use or urban knowledge and reasoning, Urban-Eval evaluates both task results and execution quality, including required tool coverage, dependency validity, and evidence traceability. Experimental results indicate that Urban-Agent reaches a 71% task success rate, 10 points above the strongest baseline. This lead holds across GPT-5-mini, Gemini-2.5-flash, DeepSeek-V4-flash, and Qwen3-235B-A22B.

</details>


### [274] [LoCA: Forward-Only LLM Tuning after One-Shot Calibration with Local Credit Assignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03020)
*Linhan Xia,Rui Liu,Zhaofeng Zhang,Yihao Wang,Binrui Shen,Shengxin Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出LoCA（Local Credit Assignment），一种两阶段的小偏移适配方法，用一次校准的前向/反向传递替代LoRA等参数高效微调中反复的端到端反向传播。LoCA通过一次探测反向传递在每个Transformer块拟合低秩映射（从最终预测误差到局部隐藏状态修正），然后利用这些映射构建逐块回归目标，并通过闭式岭回归拟合低秩适配器，后续不再需要骨干网络反向传播。在Qwen2.5（0.5B到14B）的5个判别基准上，25项任务-规模对比中有16项比LoRA取得更低评估交叉熵，GPU峰值内存降低26-29%，校准后CPU稳态内存降低36-52%，单遍时间降低43-48%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的参数高效后训练（如LoRA）虽然减少了可训练参数数量，但仍需反复通过冻结骨干网络进行端到端反向传播。每次适配步骤都需要支持反向传播的硬件，并需存储或重计算激活值。作者希望用一次性校准来替代这种重复的反向链，从而在无法进行重复反向传播的场景下也能进行前向-only的微调。

Method: LoCA包含两个阶段：(1) 校准阶段：执行一次探测反向传递，在每个Transformer块处从最终预测误差拟合一个低秩映射，将该误差映射为局部隐藏状态修正；(2) 适配阶段：利用这些拟合好的低秩映射，从正向激活构建逐块回归目标，通过闭式岭回归求解拟合低秩适配器。整个过程只需一次骨干网络反向传播，后续前向传播即可完成适配。

Result: 在Qwen2.5（0.5B-14B）的5个判别基准上，25项任务-规模对比中16项优于LoRA（更低的评估交叉熵）。全运行GPU峰值内存比LoRA低26-29%；校准后CPU稳态内存低36-52%；每遍处理时间低43-48%。此外，共享的尺度归一化候选集可复用于所有测试的Qwen2.5规模以及SmolLM2-1.7B。

Conclusion: LoCA通过将全局信用分配摊销为一次校准，使得在重复反向传播不可行的场景下（如前向-only硬件）也能进行有效的参数高效适配，在降低内存和计算开销的同时保持了与LoRA相当甚至更好的性能。

Abstract: Parameter-efficient post-training reduces the number of trainable parameters, but still requires repeated end-to-end backpropagation through the frozen backbone. Every adaptation step therefore needs backward-capable hardware and must store or recompute activations. We ask whether this repeated backward chain can be replaced by a one-time calibration. We introduce Local Credit Assignment (LoCA), a two-stage method for small-shift adaptation. One probe backward pass fits a low-rank map at each transformer block from the final prediction error to a local hidden-state correction. LoCA then reuses these maps to form blockwise regression targets from forward activations and fits low-rank adapters with closed-form ridge solves. No further backbone backward pass is required. We evaluate LoCA on five discriminative benchmarks with Qwen2.5 models from 0.5B to 14B. In 16 of 25 reported task--scale comparisons, LoCA yields lower evaluation cross-entropy than the corresponding LoRA run. Its measured full-run GPU peak, including calibration, is 26--29\% lower than LoRA's. After calibration, its CPU steady-state memory is 36--52\% lower and its per-pass time is 43--48\% lower. A shared scale-normalized candidate set is reused across all tested Qwen2.5 sizes and on SmolLM2-1.7B. LoCA thus amortizes global credit assignment into one calibration and enables later forward-only tuning when repeated backpropagation is impractical. The code associated with this paper is available \href{https://github.com/Xia12121/LoCA}{here}.

</details>


### [275] [DiffImaginE: Imagine to Verify Entity Types with Diffusio](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03025)
*Feng Zhang,Feiyu Han,Rongxin Yang,Yang Liu,Yancheng Chen,Rui Wang,Yingguang Yang,Tian Xueyun,Chongyang Zhang,Hao Zheng,Xu Kefu,Congjing Ran,Fuhai Chen,Bin Chong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DiffImaginE，一种基于条件潜在扩散推理的多模态命名实体识别（MNER）类型验证方法。它将类型验证问题建模为条件扩散去噪任务，通过去噪误差作为类型条件负对数似然的ELBO一致性代理，实现类型假设的概率化排序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的"想象-比较"验证器（如ImaginE）存在两个关键缺陷：（1）将每个（span, type）对映射为单一的预测视觉特征，将多样化的视觉实现压缩为单一原型，丢失了视觉多样性信息；（2）仅提供兼容性分数，缺乏明确的概率语义，难以对不同类型假设进行严格的概率比较。

Method: DiffImaginE将MNER类型验证形式化为条件潜在扩散推理：（1）给定span定位的视觉证据，类型条件去噪器预测注入到标准化潜变量中的噪声；（2）去噪误差作为类型条件负对数似然的ELBO一致性代理；（3）采用无分类器引导的扩散评分器，使用Min-SNR加权训练；（4）将每类型扩散分数直接监督为分类logits，学习跨噪声级别的聚合；（5）使用对偶抽样降低蒙特卡洛比较方差。

Result: 在Twitter-2015和Twitter-2017数据集上，DiffImaginE在相同编码器、辅助目标函数和评估协议下，相对于匹配的确定性ImaginE基线取得了持续改进，并通过消融实验和配对显著性检验得到验证。分析还表明无分类器引导能锐化诱导的类型后验分布，并刻画了对偶配对在相等去噪器成本下降低方差的条件。

Conclusion: DiffImaginE证明了将扩散模型作为概率化类型验证器在MNER任务中的有效性，通过保留标准多模态编码器结构、仅替换确定性验证器的方式，在维持架构兼容性的同时获得了更好的类型判别能力。

Abstract: Multimodal named entity recognition (MNER) determines whether each candidate span and entity-type hypothesis is supported by joint textual and visual evidence. Existing imagine-and-compare verifiers map each (span, type) pair to one predicted visual feature, compressing diverse visual realisations into a single prototype and providing a compatibility score without explicit probabilistic semantics. We introduce DiffImaginE, which formulates MNER type verification as conditional latent diffusion inference. Given span-localised visual evidence, a type-conditioned denoiser predicts noise injected into its standardised latent. The resulting denoising error provides an ELBO-consistent surrogate for type-conditional negative log-likelihood, allowing competing type hypotheses to be ranked by how well they explain the observation. DiffImaginE retains a standard multimodal encoder stack and replaces the deterministic verifier with a classifier-free-guided diffusion scorer trained using Min-SNR weighting. We directly supervise per-type diffusion scores as classification logits, learn aggregation across noise levels, and use antithetic sampling to reduce Monte Carlo comparison variance. Our analysis shows that classifier-free guidance sharpens the induced type posterior and characterises when antithetic pairing reduces variance at equal denoiser cost. Experiments on Twitter-2015 and Twitter-2017 show consistent gains over a matched deterministic ImaginE control under the same encoder, auxiliary objectives, and evaluation protocol, supported by ablations and paired significance tests.

</details>


### [276] [Evaluating Counterfactual Sensitivity to Patient Information in Medication-Safety Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03028)
*Zhitian Hou,Yuhang Liu,Pengkai Wang,Zeyu Liu,Guanghao Zhu,Zheng Liu,Shuo Cai,Congkai Xie,Zhijie Sang,Kun Zeng,Hongxia Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MedPIC-Bench基准测试，用于评估大语言模型在患者特定条件下应用用药安全规则的能力，通过配对的反事实问题揭示模型在条件规则应用上的系统性缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学评估多使用孤立和固定的场景，模型可能仅凭药物-风险关联记忆回答正确，而并未真正利用患者信息判断规则是否适用。这种静态评估无法反映模型在患者特定条件下用药安全推理的真实可靠性。

Method: 构建MedPIC-Bench基准，包含来源可验证的推荐和专家验证的问题，结合指南遵循问题与配对反事实问题（通过受控的患者信息改变使规则是否适用发生变化），共467个问题，沿六个临床和推理维度进行标注，并测试28个医学专用、通用和专有LLM。

Result: 所有模型在反事实问题上的表现均下降，平均准确率从63.6%降至45.1%。模型在显式患者属性直接指示已知禁忌症时表现良好，但当患者信息需要收窄或撤销安全警告时表现挣扎。模型理由常承认患者信息变化，但最终答案仍保留原有安全判断。医学专用LLM的CF表现平均落后于通用LLM。

Conclusion: MedPIC-Bench使条件规则应用可量化，并突显了静态用药安全准确率在评估患者特定可靠性方面的局限性。

Abstract: Applying a valid medication-safety rule when its patient-specific conditions are not met can produce an incorrect decision. Existing medical evaluations largely use isolated and fixed scenarios. A model may therefore answer correctly by recalling a drug-risk association without showing that it used patient information to decide whether the rule applies. To address this gap, we introduce MedPIC-Bench, a benchmark of source-verifiable recommendations and expert-validated questions for patient-specific medication-safety reasoning. It combines guideline-following questions with paired counterfactual questions in which a controlled change in patient information changes whether a rule applies. The benchmark contains 467 questions annotated along six clinical and reasoning dimensions.
